Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in probabilistic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 163 and the invariant I(A,B) = 266 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to probabilistic theory, we decompose the problem into 163 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 266. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
